## Best date plans

### 2026-11-21 — Anthony Coleman — Saturdays in November + Sofreh

**Anthony Coleman — Saturdays in November, then dinner.** Persian at Sofreh. It is a 20-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 6 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=376+9th+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&destination=75+Saint+Marks+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.viewcy.com/event/anthony_coleman__sat_11) · [Restaurant](https://www.sofrehnyc.com/) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/ny/sofreh)

### 2026-11-19 — North by Northwest — One Nite Only + Lightscape Lodge at Yellow Magnolia Café

**North by Northwest — One Nite Only, then dinner.** Seasonal American prix fixe at Lightscape Lodge at Yellow Magnolia Café. It is a 22-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 6:30 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=188+Prospect+Park+West%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&destination=Brooklyn+Botanic+Garden%2C+Palm+House+on+Lily+Pool+Terrace%3B+Lightscape+entrance+at+990+Washington+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://nitehawkcinema.com/prospectpark/movies/north-by-northwest/) · [Event booking](https://nitehawkcinema.com/prospectpark/purchase/24510003/) · [Restaurant](https://www.bbg.org/feature/lightscape/lightscape_lodge_dinners) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/new-york-ny/venues/yellow-magnolia-cafe?date=2026-11-20&seats=2)

### 2026-11-23 — SKATE at Domino Park + Stretch Pizza at Domino Park

**SKATE at Domino Park, then a meal.** New York-style pizza at Stretch Pizza at Domino Park. It is a 1-minute walk from the venue. It runs to 9 pm, so this is a late stop afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=South+3rd+Street+between+Kent+Avenue+and+River+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY&destination=320+Kent+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.dominopark.com/ice-skating) · [Event booking](https://www.dominopark.com/ice-skating/buy-tickets) · [Restaurant](https://www.stretchpizzanyc.com/location/stretch-domino-park/)

### 2026-11-21 — Sofreh + Choir Social Club

**Dinner, then Choir Social Club.** Persian at Sofreh. It is a 20-minute walk from the venue. Choir Social Club starts at 7:30 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=75+Saint+Marks+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY&destination=168+7th+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.ibeambrooklyn.com/home/2026/11/21/choir-social-club) · [Restaurant](https://www.sofrehnyc.com/) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/ny/sofreh)

### 2026-11-19 — Brasserie Boulud + Carnegie Hill Concerts New-Music Chamber Music Series

**Dinner, then Carnegie Hill Concerts New-Music Chamber Music Series.** French brasserie at Brasserie Boulud. It is 1.5 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Carnegie Hill Concerts New-Music Chamber Music Series starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=1900+Broadway%2C+New+York%2C+NY&destination=111+E+87th+Street%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10128&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.carnegiehillconcerts.org/concerts/2026/11-19-2026) · [Restaurant](https://www.brasserieboulud.com/) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/new-york-ny/venues/brasserie-boulud-nyc)

### 2026-11-18 — Altbau Bakery + Loser’s Lounge Singalong: Adele + Amy Winehouse

**Dinner, then Loser’s Lounge Singalong: Adele + Amy Winehouse.** Bakery; sourdough bread and pastries at Altbau Bakery. It is 2.8 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Loser’s Lounge Singalong: Adele + Amy Winehouse starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=184+North+8th+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY&destination=165+W+26th+Street%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10001&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://sidgolds.com/event/losers-lounge-singalong-3/) · [Restaurant](https://altbaubakery.com/)

### 2026-11-21 — Brooklyn Flea in DUMBO + SSRP

**Brooklyn Flea in DUMBO, then a meal.** Japanese ramen and small plates at SSRP. It is 1.9 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 10 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=80+Pearl+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY&destination=565+Vanderbilt+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.brooklynflea.com/) · [Restaurant](https://www.ssrpnyc.com/) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/new-york-ny/venues/ssrp)

### 2026-11-22 — Altbau Bakery + Rising Appalachia

**Dinner, then Rising Appalachia.** Bakery; sourdough bread and pastries at Altbau Bakery. It is a 5-minute walk from the venue. Doors 6:30 pm — eat first.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=184+North+8th+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY&destination=80+N+6th+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11249&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://lpr.com/lpr_events/rant226nyc/) · [Event booking](https://ci.ovationtix.com/36490/production/1279948?performanceId=11827535) · [Restaurant](https://altbaubakery.com/)

35mm Import. Made the year before Oshima’s In Realm of the Senses, Noboru Tanaka’s rendition of the Sada Abe incident rests with Junko Miyashita’s fierce portrayal of the infamous figure, whose all-consuming affair culminates in a notorious act that would grip a nation already in the throes of rising militarism.

A pinnacle of the art of filmic portraiture, Gregory Markopoulos’s GALAXIE comprises thirty short portraits of men and women from across the spectrum of the mid-1960s cultural scene in New York City. ## Where to eat

NYC dining for November 18: weekday deals, recent openings, limited-run shops and Thanksgiving menus. Casa Oaxaca is closed today; holiday listings do not imply available tables.

Dining research: 2026-11-18T10:40:33+00:00

No Michelin awards are asserted. The reviewed evidence does not establish current Michelin status for these candidates, and no Michelin directory was checked. Guide selection is not a star, and awards at chefs' former restaurants do not transfer to new venues. Supplied captures and 12 direct public-web checks informed this review; two linked PDFs could not be decoded by the web reader.

### Le Gratin: $45 two-course weekday lunch

Deals & happy hours — Le Gratin

A structured lunch near City Hall, with choices including salmon tartare, mushroom rigatoni, skate and housemade sausage.

Price: $45 two courses; $55 three courses per person  
Schedule: Monday–Friday, 11:30am–2:30pm  
Conditions: Steak tartare and cheeseburger each carry a $5 supplement; steak frites adds $8. Reservations offered, not explicitly required. Tax, gratuity, service charges and holiday exceptions not stated.  
[Source](https://legratinnyc.com/our-menu/lunch)

### Amélie: $1.50 oysters and $26 early-bird meal

Deals & happy hours — Amélie West Village

Happy hour includes oysters, $10 small plates and wine flights. The early-bird meal pairs a burger or mussels with fries and beer or wine.

Price: $1.50 oysters; $10 small plates and selected cocktails; $14 wine flights; $26 early-bird meal  
Schedule: Daily: food 4–6pm; happy-hour drinks from opening until 6pm  
Conditions: Early-bird meal includes one beer or glass of wine. Wine flights rise to $19 after 6pm. Oyster minimum, mandatory reservations, tax, gratuity and service charges not stated. Holiday exceptions unknown.  
[Source](https://ameliewinebar.com/wv/menus/happy-hour)

### Amélie: $26 two-course bistro lunch

Deals & happy hours — Amélie West Village

A separate weekday lunch option with choices including escargots, chicken paillard, croque monsieur and mussels.

Price: $26 two-course lunch; optional wine of the day $10  
Schedule: Monday–Friday, noon–3:30pm  
Conditions: Tax and gratuity excluded. Burger adds $5; steak frites and Niçoise salad add $8. One bread basket included; extra basket $3. Menu also lists desserts without clearly explaining their role in the two-course selection. Reservations offered; holiday exceptions unknown.  
[Source](https://ameliewinebar.com/wv/menus/bistro)

### Nobody Told Me: $5 snacks and $2 oysters

Deals & happy hours — Nobody Told Me — Upper West Side

An early-evening option with chicken or shrimp sliders, crispy fish tacos and masala chickpeas alongside discounted drinks.

Price: $5 listed snacks; $2 oysters; $7 draft beers; $10 wines; $12 selected cocktails  
Schedule: Daily, 3–6pm  
Conditions: Oyster minimum is four. Cocktails limited to the listed selections. Queso is $12, with chorizo +$2. Reservation requirements, tax, gratuity and service charges not stated; holiday exceptions unknown.  
[Source](https://v2-nobodytoldme.getbento.com/menu/uws-happy-hour/)

### Frenzie: late-night pizza-and-drink packages

Deals & happy hours — Frenzie

A shareable late-night alternative to ordering a pie and cocktails separately.

Price: $40 cheese pie and pitcher of Narragansett; $45 pie and two daiquiris; $50 pie and two martinis  
Schedule: Thursday–Saturday after 10pm  
Conditions: Pepperoni adds $4. Restaurant is walk-in only. Closing time, package party-size requirements, tax, gratuity and service charges not stated. Menu subject to seasonal availability; holiday exceptions unknown.  
[Source](https://www.frenzienyc.com/menus/)

### Miss Lily's: $25 boozy-brunch add-on

Deals & happy hours — Miss Lily's 7A

Weekend brunch offers an unlimited-drinks add-on with selected rum and sparkling-wine cocktails.

Price: $25 per person for the drinks add-on, plus a required Signature Dish  
Schedule: Saturday and Sunday brunch, noon–4pm  
Conditions: A Signature Dish purchase is required; $25 covers the drinks add-on, not a complete brunch. Eligible drinks are Pure Passion, Rum Punch, Coco-Lemon Drop and One Love Bellini. Duration and whole-table rules are not stated in the brunch HTML. The linked October 5 dinner PDF states that 18% gratuity is automatically added; its applicability to brunch could not be confirmed because the brunch PDF was not readable. Tax, other charges and reservation requirements remain unverified.  
[Source](https://v1-misslilys.getbento.com/east-village-7a/)

### Beatstro: 90-minute sangria-and-mimosa brunch offer

Deals & happy hours — Beatstro

A Bronx option combining Puerto Rican and Southern cooking with a clearly priced brunch beverage package.

Price: $24.95 per person for 90 minutes of unlimited Bomb Sangria and mimosa  
Schedule: During brunch; exact days and hours unknown  
Conditions: Gratuity added to all parties; percentage not stated. Food purchase requirement, food inclusion, tax and other service charges not stated. Official events page encourages reservations for events and groups of five or more. Holiday availability unconfirmed.  
[Source](https://www.beatstro.com/brunch-menu)

### Amélie Upper West Side: $52 Thanksgiving set

Special menus — Amélie Upper West Side

Three courses of pumpkin soup, stuffed turkey ballotine and pumpkin pie, with an à la carte alternative.

Price: $52 three courses; turkey ballotine à la carte $34  
Schedule: Thanksgiving Day; special service hours not stated  
Conditions: Turkey contains mushrooms, cranberries and chestnuts. Reservations offered; availability untested. Tax, gratuity, service charges and whole-table requirements not stated.  
Event date: 2026-11-26  
[Source](https://ameliewinebar.com/uws/menus/thanksgiving)

### Cafe D'Alsace: $95 Thanksgiving prix fixe

Special menus — Cafe D'Alsace

A holiday menu with turkey, salmon, braised short rib or mushroom risotto, plus appetizer and dessert choices.

Price: $95 three courses; turkey plate for children 12 and under $32  
Schedule: Thanksgiving, 2–9pm  
Conditions: Children's price is for the turkey plate, not explicitly a three-course meal. Reservation requirements, tax, gratuity and service charges not stated; availability untested.  
Event date: 2026-11-26  
[Source](https://www.cafedalsace.com/menus/)

### Le Rock: Thanksgiving with turkey, halibut or filet

Special menus — Le Rock

The current holiday menu includes bread service, an appetizer, entrée, shared traditional sides and dessert.

Price: $185; official menu labels it 'Three + Courses'  
Schedule: November 26; exact holiday seating hours unknown  
Conditions: Reservations linked through SevenRooms; availability untested. Tax, gratuity and service charges not stated in readable official menu. Current menu differs from some dishes described in Eater's preview.  
Event date: 2026-11-26  
[Source](https://www.lerocknyc.com/menus/)

### Russian Tea Room: three-course Thanksgiving

Special menus — The Russian Tea Room

Holiday choices include borscht, turkey, salmon-and-lobster kulebyaka, mushroom stroganoff and medovik.

Price: $145 per person; $95 for children 12 and under  
Schedule: November 26; precise meal seating hours not verified  
Conditions: Three-course menu. Reservations offered through OpenTable; availability untested. Tax, gratuity and service charges not stated on the menu.  
Event date: 2026-11-26  
Published: 2026-10-29  
[Source](https://russiantearoomnyc.com/thanksgiving/)

### La Compagnie Flatiron: Alexia Duchêne's seasonal collaboration

Special menus — La Compagnie Flatiron

Consulting chef Alexia Duchêne's French-inspired seasonal dishes accompany the Flatiron wine bar's wine selection.

Schedule: Monday–Wednesday 5pm–midnight; Thursday–Saturday 5pm–1am; Sunday 5–11pm; kitchen cutoff unknown  
Conditions: An ongoing chef collaboration launched November 8, not a one-night dinner or discount. The restaurant links a food PDF, but the web reader returned undecoded PDF content; food prices remain unverified. The separately captured by-the-glass PDF is a beverage list, not evidence of food prices. Reservations are offered; holiday exceptions are unknown.  
From: 2026-11-08  
[Source](https://www.experimentalgroup.com/new-york/la-compagnie-flatiron)

### Brasserie Boulud: French classics near Lincoln Center

New openings — Brasserie Boulud

Daniel Boulud consolidated three former Lincoln Center-area venues into a new brasserie serving charcuterie, seafood and French classics.

Price: Official menu: classic burger $25; trout $30; half rotisserie chicken $39; filet-mignon steak frites $48  
Schedule: Tuesday–Sunday, 4:30–10:30pm  
Conditions: Official current hours show dinner only; do not assume breakfast or lunch from earlier previews. Reservations offered. Holiday exceptions and additional charges unknown.  
Published: 2026-11-03  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-nyc-restaurants-heatmap)

### Āzar opens a Persian street-grill counter

New openings — Āzar

Soroosh Golbabae, formerly of Sofreh and Eyval, serves housemade lavash wraps with fillings such as chicken fesenjoon and ghormeh sabzi.

Schedule: Wednesday–Sunday, noon–5pm; closed Monday and Tuesday  
Conditions: Fast-casual format. Prices and reservation policy not verified. No holiday closure notice appeared on the official page.  
Opened: 2026-11-07  
Published: 2026-11-12  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/maps/nyc-new-restaurant-openings-2026)

### Altbau Bakery: bread and pastries from Chad Robertson

New openings — Altbau Bakery

Tartine co-founder Chad Robertson's bakery includes an on-site grain mill and pastries from former Radio Bakery baker Wei Si Phua.

Schedule: Wednesday–Sunday, 9am until sold out  
Conditions: Sellout-dependent service. Prices, reservation policy and Thanksgiving exceptions unverified. The November 7–8 visiting-chef event has ended and is not recommended as upcoming.  
Published: 2026-11-05  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-restaurants-brooklyn-heatmap)

### SSRP: tonkotsu, mazemen and Japanese sandwiches

New openings — SSRP

A new ramen-and-izakaya option with tonkotsu, mazemen, Japanese sandwiches and Nagoya-style chicken wings.

**On-sale watch:** December 13 is the last date on the promoter's official 2026 NYC residency schedule. No later residency performance is listed.
[Official](https://youarehere.bowerypresents.com/) · [Tickets / booking](https://www.axs.com/events/1636873/lcd-soundsystem-tickets?skin=bowerypresents)
**Sale/deadline:** 2026-12-13

#### [SIX: final Broadway performance](https://sixonbroadway.com/)

2027-01-03 · 7:00 PM · theater · Standard ticket prices unverified · Lena Horne Theatre · Theater District · Manhattan · Score 51.0  
The pop-concert musical gives Henry VIII's six wives the microphone to retell their histories. Its official production site announces the Broadway run's final performance on January 3.
**Timing:** The official calendar lists 2pm and 7pm performances on closing day; this item identifies the final 7pm performance.
**Practical note:** Standard prices and final-performance inventory were not verified. The published $35 student rush requires valid student identification and is not an advance booking option.
**Why now:** The announced Broadway closure creates a real visit-by date for holiday planning.
**On-sale watch:** The official site explicitly announces January 3 as Broadway's final performance date; its ticket calendar ends with the 7pm show.
[Official](https://sixonbroadway.com/) · [Tickets / booking](https://tickets.broadwaydirect.com/tickets/series/719897/2211791) · [Discovered via Playbill Productions](https://playbill.com/shows/broadway)
**Sale/deadline:** 2027-01-03T19:00:00-05:00
